The AI Morning Newspaper

Your daily briefing on artificial intelligence, from the world's leading tech sources
2026-10-12
21 sources · 389 articles · 4,842,321 characters

In the Last 24 Hours

Meta's Superintelligence Lab has finally shipped its first frontier models, Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full, trained at the new Prometheus data center and benchmarking close to GPT-6 Astra with a heavy emphasis on active memory for long-term user retention. On the regulation front, Microsoft President Brad Smith urged the UK to take the lead on global AI safety standards, arguing that voluntary measures alone cannot manage the cybersecurity risks of advanced models. Meanwhile, the startup ecosystem continues to churn: Blueprints AI converts 2D sketches into code-compliant construction blueprints in minutes, former Plaud executive Peter Mo raised $26 million for Ambi, an always-listening AI assistant, and Y Combinator-backed Agent37 launched low-cost sandboxes for running AI agents at under ten percent of typical market prices. WindBorne Systems secured a contract worth up to $49 million from the US Air Force for atmospheric data collected by its autonomous weather balloons, feeding empirical readings directly into deep-learning forecasting models. A provocative essay by Diomidis Spinellis argues that AI's superior performance and 24/7 availability will render traditional knowledge work obsolete, turning thinking itself into a leisure hobby.

Apple is running a packed week across its entire hardware and services portfolio. The company confirmed the HomeView, a Siri-powered smart home hub with an A18 chip available in countertop and wall-mounted models, alongside a new Apple TV 4K featuring an A19 chip, built-in microphone for hands-free Siri, and an N1 wireless chip. iPhone Duo pre-order preparation has opened with Apple reportedly increasing supply chain orders by 30 percent to nearly 10 million units by year-end, while a second-generation iPhone Air with a dual-camera system and 2-nanometer A20 Pro chip is reportedly targeting spring 2027. On the content and accessories side, Apple is launching Remnants, the first immersive horror film for Vision Pro, directing viewers inside Woldmere Castle, and has confirmed a Beats x Pokemon collaboration on a special edition Pill speaker. A massive sales event offers $600 off the 24GB M5 Pro MacBook Pro and up to $150 off M6 Mac mini models, while Indie Games Week spotlights seven new titles including Sifu and No Man's Sky. A new macOS app called ShyGlass uses AirPods motion sensors to blur your screen when you look away, though it requires Screen Recording permissions that present a notable security trade-off.

Google is making a sweeping push into personal health with the launch of the Fitbit Edge tracker at $179.99, major Fitbit Air updates expanding automatic activity detection to over 100 sports, and a multi-year partnership with Flo Health to integrate cycle tracking with Google's wearables and coaching services. The company is also enlisting high-profile athletes and creators including Stephen Curry and MrBeast to refine its AI-powered health tools through real-world feedback, while its broader research division applies AI to genetic diagnoses, drug repurposing, cancer detection, and disease outbreak forecasting. On the developer side, a programmer successfully launched a commercial web product using C and CGI, a deliberate throwback that challenges modern framework assumptions. TechCrunch Disrupt 2026 kicks off tomorrow at Moscone West in San Francisco, bringing over 10,000 industry leaders together for three days of networking and the Startup Battlefield competition, with final ticket discounts still available.

Meta 嘅 Superintelligence Lab 終於推出咗首批前沿模型 Muse Wonder 同 Muse Wonder Full，喺 Prometheus 数据中心训练，跑分接近 GPT-6 Astra，主打「主动记忆」能力，可以长期记住用户嘅上下文。微软总裁 Brad Smith 呼吁英国带头制定全球 AI 监管标准，佢认为自愿性措施根本唔够应付先进模型带来嘅网络安全风险。创业圈方面，Blueprints AI 用生成式 AI 将二维草图几分钟内变成符合建筑规范嘅施工蓝图，前 Plaud 高管 Peter Mo 为 Ambi 筹到 2600 万美金做一款全天候聆听嘅 AI 助手，Y Combinator 孵化嘅 Agent37 推出咗成本低于市场十份一嘅 AI agent 沙盒。WindBorne Systems 攞到美国空军最高 4900 万美金合约，用自动气象气球收集大气数据喂畀深度学习天气模型。仲有一篇好犀利嘅文章话 AI 会将人类认知劳动彻底取代，将来「思考」会变成同打猎、书法一样嘅消遣爱好。

Apple 呢个星期真系全线开花。HomeView 智能家庭中枢正式确认，搭载 A18 芯片，有台面款同壁挂款；新版 Apple TV 4K 用上 A19 芯片、内置麦克风做免提 Siri、加咗 N1 无线芯片。iPhone Duo 预购准备已经开放，Apple 据报将供应链订单加码三成，目标年底前生产接近一千万部；第二代 iPhone Air 据报会配双镜头同 2 纳米 A20 Pro 芯片，预计 2027 年春季推出。内容同配件方面，Apple 将喺 Vision Pro 推出首部沉浸式恐怖电影 Remnants，由 Ti West 执导，观众会置身 Woldmere Castle 入面；Beats 同 Pokemon 嘅联名 Pill 音箱都正式官宣。大减价方面，24GB M5 Pro MacBook Pro 直减 600 美金，M6 Mac mini 最多减 150 美金，Indie Games Week 仲有 Sifu、No Man's Sky 等七款独立游戏上架。另外有款叫 ShyGlass 嘅 macOS app，用 AirPods 动作感应器喺你转头嗰刻自动模糊屏幕，不过佢要 Screen Recording 权限，隐私上其实有取舍。

Google 喺个人健康领域做咗一次大动作：Fitbit Edge 追踪器以 179.99 美金登场，Fitbit Air 大幅更新将自动运动侦测扩展到超过一百种运动，仲同 Flo Health 达成多年战略合作，将经期追踪数据同 Google 穿戴设备同健康教练服务打通。Google 仲拉埋 Stephen Curry 同 MrBeast 等名人入伙，用真实世界反馈嚟打磨 AI 健康工具；研究部门方面，佢哋将 AI 应用喺基因诊断、药物再利用、癌症检测同疫情预测上。开发者圈有个有趣嘅故事：有人用 C 语言加 CGI 成功上线咗一个商业 web 产品，摆明系向现代框架框架宣战。TechCrunch Disrupt 2026 听日喺旧金山 Moscone West 开幕，超过一万名业界领袖齐聚三日，Startup Battlefield 大赛同埋最后嘅门票折扣都仲赶得切。

I Found a $90k Remote Code Execution Vulnerability in Google

https://simonkoeck.com/writeups/google-devsite-flask-session-pickle-rce📅 2026-10-12 15:28 UTC+8▲ 102 ptsHN discussion

A security researcher discovered a critical remote code execution vulnerability in Google’s developer sites, earning a $90,000 bounty after proving that Flask session cookies leaked the OAuth client secret used to sign them. By extracting this secret from the cookie payload, the attacker could forge valid sessions and trigger unsafe pickle deserialization, allowing arbitrary code execution across seven major Google domains. The issue was resolved by rotating secrets, implementing proper key management, and replacing pickle with a safer data format.

一名安全研究員喺 Google 開發者網站搵到一個嚴重嘅遠程代碼執行漏洞，因為佢發現 Flask session cookie 竟然洩露咗用來簽名嘅 OAuth client secret，最終獲得九萬美元獎金。只要提取呢個 secret，攻擊者就可以偽造有效 session 並觸發不安全嘅 pickle 反序列化，從而喺七個 Google 域名上執行任意代碼。Google 已經透過更換密鑰、改善密鑰管理同改用更安全嘅數據格式嚟修復呢個問題。

After a 60-year scientific pursuit, researchers have identified a specific 5-kilobase deletion in the Arhgap36 gene on the X chromosome as the cause of orange fur in cats. This genetic mutation explains the distinct sex-linked inheritance patterns, such as why most orange cats are male and why females often display patchwork calico or tortoiseshell coats. The discovery resolves a long-standing mystery in feline genetics while confirming that the trait has no negative impact on the cats' health or cognition.

經過六十年的研究，科學家終於發現貓的橙色毛髮是由X染色體上Arhgap36基因的一段DNA缺失所導致。這個發現解釋了為何橙色貓多為雄性，以及為何雌性貓常出現三花或玳瑁色的斑塊狀毛色。研究證實這基因變異不會影響貓的健康或智力，只是單純決定了牠們那身引人注目的火紅色皮毛。

The keys to the Internet change on October 11. Are you ready?

https://blog.cloudflare.com/root-ksk-2024-rollover/📅 2026-10-07 01:50 UTC+8

On October 11, 2026, the DNS root will undergo its second-ever key-signing key rollover, replacing KSK-2017 with KSK-2024 to maintain the chain of trust for DNSSEC. While most website operators require no action, administrators of validating resolvers must ensure their systems trust the new key to prevent widespread website unavailability. Cloudflare users can verify their readiness through a specific test that checks if their resolver correctly recognizes the new trust anchor.

2026年10月11日，DNS根區將進行第二次金鑰簽名金鑰輪換，由KSK-2024取代舊有的KSK-2017，以維持DNSSEC的信任鏈。雖然大部分網站營運商無需採取行動，但運行驗證解析器的管理員必須確保系統信任新金鑰，否則可能導致大範圍網站無法存取。Cloudflare用戶可透過特定測試檢查其解析器是否已正確識別新的信任錨點。

MCP 喺 2026 年 7 月推出咗重大更新，將協議核心改為無狀態模式，徹底取消咗舊有嘅 session 握手同 sticky routing，令遠程伺服器部署更符合 AWS Well-Architected 嘅最佳實踐。呢個改變透過將狀態管理轉移到客戶端標識符，實現咗原生嘅水平擴展同簡化基礎設施，不過團隊必須保留舊有嘅 session 基礎設施，直至所有 2026 年前嘅客戶端完全退役為止。

RDMA: The Reason Remote Storage Feels Like It's Plugged Into Your Motherboard

https://hackernoon.com/rdma-the-reason-remote-storage-feels-like-its-plugged-into-your-motherboard📅 2026-10-12 22:10 UTC+8

RDMA technology eliminates the traditional network latency bottleneck by enabling direct memory access between computers without CPU involvement, allowing remote storage to perform nearly as fast as local drives. This shift is critical for modern AI infrastructure, where minimizing I/O delays prevents expensive GPU clusters from idling during high-bandwidth tasks like model checkpointing and data loading.

RDMA 技術透過讓電腦之間直接存取記憶體而無需 CPU 參與，消除了傳統網絡延遲的瓶頸，令遠程儲存的速度幾乎與本地硬碟一樣快。對於現代 AI 基礎設施而言，這至關重要，因為減少 I/O 延遲能防止昂貴的 GPU 集群在進行模型檢查點保存和數據加載等高帶寬任務時閒置浪費。

A Short History of the Barcode

https://hackernoon.com/a-short-history-of-the-barcode📅 2026-10-12 05:44 UTC+8

In 1948, supermarket executive Sam Friedland sought a machine-readable system to streamline checkout processes, leading students Bernard Silver and Joseph Woodland to invent the barcode. Inspired by Morse code, they developed a spatial encoding method using lines and spaces, patenting a bullseye design in 1952 that could be scanned from any angle. Although the technology was too advanced for the era’s bulky and expensive computers, their foundational work solved the critical problem of automated product identification.

1948年，超市高管山姆·弗里德兰为了解决收银效率低下的问题，委托学生开发一种机器可读的产品编码系统。伯纳德·西尔弗和约瑟夫·伍德兰受摩尔斯电码启发，发明了利用线条和空白进行空间编码的条形码，并于1952年获得了专利。尽管当时笨重且昂贵的电脑使得这项技术无法立即投入实际应用，但他们的发明为后来的自动化零售奠定了重要基础。

Five Supabase Backup Failure Modes and How to Restore-Test for Them

https://hackernoon.com/five-supabase-backup-failure-modes-and-how-to-restore-test-for-them📅 2026-10-12 05:12 UTC+8

Supabase backups often appear successful while silently failing to capture critical data, such as database rows, storage files, or recent updates due to common configuration defaults. These hidden failures can lead to catastrophic data loss during restoration, making regular, hands-on restore testing essential to verify that backups are actually usable.

Supabase 嘅備份雖然睇落正常，但好多時其實冇備份到實際數據、檔案或者最新嘅更新，因為好多預設設定都會導致備份不完整。呢啲隱蔽嘅問題會令恢復時出現嚴重數據遺失，所以一定要定期實際做恢復測試，先至可以確保備份真係用得著。

Read full article →

How We Caught an LLM Scoring Bug and Fixed It Without Compromising Our Research

https://hackernoon.com/how-we-caught-an-llm-scoring-bug-and-fixed-it-without-compromising-our-research📅 2026-10-12 04:35 UTC+8

A research team discovered that a 100% failure rate in their LLM benchmark was caused by overly strict parsing code rather than model collapse, leading them to implement a transparent fix that corrected format errors while preserving genuine content failures. This correction revealed that the model actually performed consistently across prompts, preventing a misleading conclusion that structured prompting caused total failure. The incident also highlighted how hidden reasoning tokens in free-tier APIs can truncate outputs, mimicking poor model performance if token budgets are not properly managed.

研究團隊發現基準測試中某模型百分百失敗並非模型崩潰，而是解析程式碼過於嚴苛所致，他們透過修正格式錯誤但保留內容失敗的方式，還原了模型真實且一致的表現。這次修正避免了誤導性的結論，同時揭示了免費版 API 中隱藏的推理代幣會截斷輸出，若未妥善設定代幣預算，容易被誤判為模型品質問題。

Async GRPO with LoRA across HF Jobs: a bucket, a proxy, and no NCCL

https://huggingface.co/blog/asyncgrpo-lora-hfjobs📅 2026-09-10 08:00 UTC+8

Engineers have developed a method to train LoRA adapters asynchronously across separate Hugging Face Jobs by syncing only small adapter files via a shared Storage Bucket, effectively bypassing the need for NCCL communication. This architecture utilizes a proxy server to manage KV cache routing and adapter broadcasting, allowing training and inference to run on distinct machines without shared local disks. The approach significantly reduces training time from over three hours to under an hour for 500 steps, demonstrating a scalable solution for reinforcement learning workflows that avoids the bandwidth bottlenecks of full model synchronization.

工程師開發咗一種新方法，透過共用 Storage Bucket 只同步細小嘅 LoRA adapter 檔案，從而喺獨立嘅 Hugging Face Jobs 之間進行非同步訓練，成功避開咗 NCCL 通訊需求。呢個架構利用代理伺服器管理 KV cache 路由同 adapter 廣播，令訓練同推理可以喺唔同機器上運行而唔需要共用本地磁碟。呢種方法將 500 步嘅訓練時間由超過三小時大幅縮短至一小時以內，展示咗一個可擴展嘅強化學習工作流解決方案，有效避免咗完整模型同步所帶來嘅頻寬瓶頸。

Read full article →

Building Git infrastructure for agent-scale development

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/building-git-infrastructure-for-agent-scale-development/📅 2026-10-07 04:57 UTC+8

GitHub is fundamentally rebuilding its Git infrastructure to handle the explosive growth of agentic software development, where commit volumes have surged to billions monthly and write throughput demands have increased nearly fivefold. This architectural shift addresses critical bottlenecks in the current system, specifically the conflict between adding read replicas for scale and maintaining write speed, by separating durability from scalability to support sustained concurrent operations. By engineering for the most extreme workloads, GitHub aims to provide a faster, more resilient foundation that benefits all developers while preserving essential controls like branch protections and audit logs.

GitHub 正徹底重建其 Git 基礎設施，以應對代理式軟件開發帶來的爆炸性增長，目前每月提交量已達數十億，寫入吞吐量需求更激增近五倍。這次架構轉變旨在解決現有系統中的關鍵瓶頸，特別是將持久性與擴展性分離，以支持高併發操作，從而避免增加讀取副本會拖慢寫入速度的問題。通過針對最極端的工作負載進行工程優化，GitHub 旨在為所有開發者提供更快速、更具韌性的基礎設施，同時保留分支保護和審計日誌等重要控制功能。

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/improving-site-performance-by-shipping-more-css/📅 2026-09-25 23:00 UTC+8

GitHub’s Primer team migrated from CSS-in-JS to CSS Modules to resolve significant performance bottlenecks, achieving a 55% reduction in server-side rendering time and a 25% decrease in component initialization. This large-scale effort involved a gradual, feature-flagged rollout and the systematic removal of thousands of `sx` props, ultimately enabling the complete deprecation of `styled-components` across the platform.

GitHub 嘅 Primer 團隊為咗解決效能問題，由 CSS-in-JS 轉用 CSS Modules，成功將伺服器端渲染時間減少 55%，組件初始化時間亦減少 25%。呢個大規模遷移過程採用漸進式策略，逐步移除數千個 `sx` 屬性，最終徹底淘汰咗 `styled-components`。

Engineers at GitHub rebuilt the pull request view in the Copilot app to maintain smooth performance when handling massive diffs containing over a million changed lines. 85 words

Don’t stop early: Case-folding source code at memory speed

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/dont-stop-early-case-folding-source-code-at-memory-speed/📅 2026-08-01 00:00 UTC+8

GitHub engineers optimized case folding for their code search engine by eliminating early-exit branches in the ASCII processing loop, achieving memory-bandwidth speeds of over 45 GiB/s. This counterintuitive approach, which prioritizes branch-free vectorization over stopping at the first non-ASCII byte, significantly outperforms standard library methods and is now available as an open-source Rust crate. The optimization is critical for handling the massive scale of GitHub’s 480TB index, where even basic operations must run at maximum efficiency.

GitHub 的工程團隊為了提升代碼搜尋引擎的效率，在處理 ASCII 字元時移除了提前退出的分支判斷，讓大小寫轉換速度達到每秒 45 GiB 的記憶體頻寬極限。這種反直覺的做法證明，在熱迴圈中避免分支並讓編譯器進行向量化，比遇到非 ASCII 字元就中斷處理還要快得多。這個已開源的 Rust 套件能大幅加速 GitHub 上超過 480TB 代碼庫的索引建立與搜尋過程。
